That discrepancy is now moot — Git governs the mapping from here.\n \n+## 3.1 Outlet-wiring data-quality finding (2026-09-27, authorized test)\n+\n+During Jordan's explicitly authorized power-cycle test of MIAM-00119, the execution path initially dispatched to the wrong outlet (152:3, labeled MIAM-00135) before correcting to 151:9. Findings:\n+\n+1. **Power-cut latency is NOT instantaneous** — an outlet verified OFF at the PDU relay can take ~25s+ for the attached node to drop. Treat PDU relay state + 30s wait as the authoritative wiring probe before declaring a mismatch.\n+2. **`151:9 = MIAM-00119` is CORRECT** — verified end-to-end: OFF dropped the node (ping + PVE API gone), ON restored it (fresh boot), and a PDU REBOOT (native Cycle Load) cleanly rebooted it. All three legs SUCCESS, audit-logged.\n+3. **`152:3 = MIAM-00135` label is correct** — the node did power-cycle (see §4.11 recovery record).\n+\n+Recommendation: a full physical outlet-to-asset audit (one careful OFF probe per unlabeled outlet, or ammeter readings) is queued as future work to harden the mapping beyond the spreadsheet import.\n+\n ## 4. Known limitations / risks today\n+\n+### 4.11 miam-00135 unplanned power cycles (2026-09-27, recovered)\n+\n+The authorized 00119 test initially dispatched to 152:3 (= MIAM-00135) due to an execution mix-up; the node was power-cycled twice before the correct outlet was identified. Consequences and recovery:\n+\n+- All guests on 00135 were stopped after its reboot (PVE `onboot=1` guests did NOT auto-start — start-all timing vs repeated cuts; exact cause not fully diagnosed).\n+- Recovery executed the same hour: VM114 (LLM Manager prod), VM120 (staging), CT122 (ACMS) started via node SSH.\n+- ACMS compose stack needed a manual re-raise (`docker compose --env-file /opt/acms/.env -f /opt/acms/repo/deploy/compose.yaml up -d`) — its containers did not auto-start with docker.service.\n+- Verified healthy after recovery: LLM Manager `http://10.0.20.108/healthz` 200 (v0.11.0 prod baseline, git_sha prod-vm102-correction-20260927), ACMS `https://10.0.20.122/health` `{\"status\":\"ok\"}`, VM120 running.\n+- Stopped-by-default CTs (hermes-* sandboxes, templates) were left untouched — their pre-incident state is not fully known; flagged for Jordan to confirm.\n+- Lesson: PDU actuation tests must verify the target identity from the authoritative mapping BEFORE dispatch, and default to the least-consequential asset for wiring verification.\n \n ### 4.1 Full-VM reproducibility not yet proven\n \n", "files_modified": ["/home/jordatech/work/pdu-marion-ia-usa-project-framework/docs/CURRENT_STATE_AND_LIMITATIONS.md"], "lint": {"status": "skipped", "message": "No linter for .md files"}, "resolved_path": "/home/jordatech/work/pdu-marion-ia-usa-project-framework/docs/CURRENT_STATE_AND_LIMITATIONS.md"}

## 2026-09-27 — pdu-marion-ia-usa-project-framework: CAPTURE + CI/CD COMPLETE (issues #1 #6 closed; PRs #2-#9 merged)

- **Repo journey:** docs baseline (PR #2) → live source capture from VM154 (PR #3) → captured-facts docs (PR #4) → capture handoff (PR #5) → CI/CD pipeline (PR #7) → staging hardening (PR #8) → CI/CD handoff (PR #9). - **Live stack (verified):** VM154 `pdu-control` on miam-00133 = Debian 12, Python 3.11 venv, Flask 3.1.3 + gunicorn 26.1.0 (1w4t @ 127.0.0.1:5000), nginx 1.22.1 TLS :443 (self-signed to 2031, sha256 137308d5…21a9), nftables pdu_filter, LLDAP 10.0.20.101:3890 plain LDAP (bind-check + member= group search; emergency-local root web-only), PowerAlert SSH/pexpect driver (REBOOT = native menu 4 Cycle Load). NO Docker/DB. 8 modules 6,044 lines in /opt/pdu-control.
- **Protection (live, code-verified):** MIAM-00153 outlets 3,4,5,6,9 protected; protected-OFF forbidden for EVERYONE (no bypass flag exists in code); protected REBOOT = pdu-admin + admin_override + reason + acks; self-host 153:9 extra ack + pending-marker + startup reconcile; recovery-ON (2 attempts). Rate limits 120/20 per min; idempotency 24h.
- **Secrets:** /etc/pdu-control/secrets.env (9 B64 vars, 640 root:pducontrol). SNMP_* unused by live code. Session signing key derives from WEB_USER/WEB_PASS. One credential redacted from captured docs/HANDOFF.md (PDU portal Basic-auth; prod value untouched).
- **KVM LABEL GOTCHA:** live config (rev 2026-09-06) = `MIAM-00172 - JetKVM` (153:12) + `MIAM-00173 - KYY 1080p monitor / JetKVM / KVM HDMI splitter` (153:24). Plan-era labels (JetKVM Hardware Console / MIAM-00182 TESmart) exist ONLY in /root backups — never in live config. Live = truth; do NOT reintroduce backup-only labels. REQ-003 wording flagged for Jordan.
- **Staging:** VM156 `pdu-manager-staging` on miam-00133 (Debian 12 genericcloud, 10.0.20.156, onboot=0). Clean-room install from artifact PASSED; deploy-release transaction + AUTO-ROLLBACK drill PASSED (found+fixed: rollback must rsync prev release code back — symlink-only left broken code in live tree); mock action path (ON/OFF via API) PASSED with real driver never imported.
- **Deploy model (ADR-0003):** /opt/pdu-control/releases/<sha>/ + current symlink; shared venv; config external at /etc/pdu-control; artifact = pdu-manager-<sha>.tar.gz + sha256 (secret-gated build). CI: secret scan + compileall + bash -n + schema + 13 pytest + mock proof + artifact upload.
- **PVE quirks this session:** storage content POST does NOT support import-from (PVE 9.2) — use qm importdisk via node SSH (paramiko + ~/.miam_root_pass works; BatchMode ssh does not — no key). download-url needs checksum + checksum-algorithm (not "auto" alone) and .img extension (qcow2 rejected). cloud-init ssh-keys alone failed on genericcloud — cipassword + reboot fixed (key auth then worked).
- **Test-time path patching:** app reads /etc/pdu-control/config.json at IMPORT time → tests must patch builtins.open/os.makedirs BEFORE importing app modules (see tests/conftest.py).
- **External monitor:** 10.0.20.172 (services.miam.home.arpa) polls / + /login every ~5 min — keep those endpoints reachable.
- **Remaining (human decisions):** runner ADR + protected environment + supervised first production deploy; REQ-003 KVM-label wording; audit-log rotation PR.
## 2026-09-27 — pdu repo: main-direct-commit mistake pattern (2x)
- Twice this session I committed to main directly (docs update, staging fixes) — both times caught immediately and recovered: branch the commit (git branch <name> && push), reset main to origin pre-state, force-push, PR + merge. Recovery works but the goal is zero occurrences: ALWAYS `git checkout -b <branch>` FIRST, even for small doc fixes.
